# Stage 03
## Blocks
| Block | Guide section | Learning points / contexts | Cards | ID range |
|---|---|---|---:|---|
| B1a | 6.4, 5 | Verb, adjective and preposition government: helping, trusting, waiting, interest, satisfaction, dependence, reasons and limitations. Familiar abstract and practical problems; lexical governor stays visible. P; A2 case functions, lexical sense determines construction. | 100 | ru-03-0001–ru-03-0100 |
| B1b | 6.4, 5 | Aspect in narrative sequence, background/process, repeated events, intention, interrupted attempt and resulting state. Work mishaps, visits, travel and reports; temporal links/negation preserve event relation. P; A2 aspect packages. | 100 | ru-03-0101–ru-03-0200 |
| B1c | 6.4, 5 | Literal при- arrival and у- departure, on foot/vehicle/flight/carrying/leading; then common arrival/departure extensions only with a concrete lexicalized sense. Meetings, stations, workplace and home; origin/destination and result explicit. P; unprefixed pairs. | 100 | ru-03-0201–ru-03-0300 |
| B1d | 6.4, 5 | Literal в-/вы- entering/exiting and под- approaching. Buildings, rooms, streets, transport and deliveries; boundary/approach cue distinguishes arrival from entry. P; B1c and A2 motion. Limited common extended senses follow their literal prerequisites within block. | 100 | ru-03-0301–ru-03-0400 |
| B1e | 6.4, 5 | пере- crossing/transferring, за- brief visit or movement behind/into, до- reaching a limit. Crossing a road, transfers, errands, route endpoints; path and purpose visible. P; B1c–d. Separate prefix senses and common extensions; do not equate each prefix with one English preposition. | 100 | ru-03-0401–ru-03-0500 |
| B1f | 6.4 | Reflexive/passive and lexical -ся meanings, agent/affected reference, common impersonal necessity/possibility/evaluation. Services, notices, process descriptions, feelings and decisions. P; A2 reflexives and case frames; keep actor and patient interpretable. | 100 | ru-03-0501–ru-03-0600 |
| B1g | 6.4 | Conditional, unreal/hypothetical choices, polite possibility and consequence. Plans, alternatives, regrets and recommendations; condition/time frame explicit. P; A2 бы and clause relations. | 100 | ru-03-0601–ru-03-0700 |
| B1h | 6.4 | Reported statements/questions/requests and source attribution, person/time reference. Messages from colleagues, friends and public announcements; source and referent visible. P; indirect questions, imperatives, familiar time/aspect. No invented quotation from real people. | 100 | ru-03-0701–ru-03-0800 |
| B1i | 6.4 | Relative clauses with который in needed case, subject versus object, antecedent agreement. Describing people, objects, places and proposals; one antecedent and visible governor. P; A2 declension and B1 government. | 100 | ru-03-0801–ru-03-0900 |
| B1j | 6.4, 9 | Frequent present/past active and passive participles, full/short recognition, agreement and agent relation. Notices, simple reports, damaged/delivered objects. R dominant, P only frequent controlled forms; familiar relative clauses and passive meanings. No unintroduced dense literary syntax. | 100 | ru-03-0901–ru-03-1000 |
| B1k | 6.4, 13 | Adverbial participles in recognition/controlled written use: shared subject, simultaneous/prior action, negation and common fixed forms. Written messages and brief narrative; clear controller/time relation. R/P labeled; B1 participial/aspect prerequisites. Reject dangling subjects. | 100 | ru-03-1001–ru-03-1100 |
| B1l | 6.4, 8 | Comparison, degree/intensity, quantity and impersonal stance in explanations; distinct lexical collocations for evaluation. Choosing services, evaluating results, contrasting routines. P; A2 comparatives and B1 case frames. Preserve comparison scope. | 100 | ru-03-1101–ru-03-1200 |
| B1m | 6.4, 5, 10 | Topic/focus word order and conversational particles же, ведь, ли, разве, даже, только in distinct functions. Corrections, expectations, surprise and replies; brief contrast/question makes focus recoverable. P; neutral syntax and familiar response vocabulary. Never hide an arbitrary shuffled clause. | 100 | ru-03-1201–ru-03-1300 |
| B1n | 6.4, 8, 12 | Neutral versus colloquial lexical alternatives, response formulas, polite repair and formal/informal messages. Friends, service encounters, familiar abstract discussion and source reports; relationship/purpose explicit. P standard forms; colloquial recognition labeled where needed. Prerequisite B1 blocks and A1 ты/вы. | 100 | ru-03-1301–ru-03-1400 |
## Cards
| ID | Block | Hidden learning point |

| ru-03-0001 | B1a | teacher; Dative singular masculine animate. Обязан takes the dative for the person to whom the speaker owes his success. |
| ru-03-0002 | B1a | move to a new home; Instrumental singular masculine after с. Помочь с introduces the task in which someone helps. |
| ru-03-0003 | B1a | translation; Prepositional singular masculine after при. При переводе identifies the activity during which the dictionary helps. |
| ru-03-0004 | B1a | doctor; Dative singular masculine animate; своему agrees with it. Доверять takes the dative for the person trusted. |
| ru-03-0005 | B1a | rumors; Dative plural. Верить takes the dative for what one believes; слухи here means rumors. |
| ru-03-0006 | B1a | colleagues; Prepositional plural animate after в. Уверен в ком-то expresses confidence in people. |
| ru-03-0007 | B1a | result; Prepositional singular masculine after в. Сомневаться в introduces what one doubts. |
| ru-03-0008 | B1a | reply; Genitive singular masculine. Ждать can take the genitive for an anticipated reply. |
| ru-03-0009 | B1a | meeting; Genitive singular feminine. Ждать takes the genitive here for an anticipated event; с нетерпением expresses eagerness. |
| ru-03-0010 | B1a | support; Accusative singular feminine after на; вашу agrees with it. Рассчитывать на introduces the support one counts on. |
| ru-03-0011 | B1a | discount; Accusative singular feminine after на; такую agrees with it. Надеяться на introduces the desired outcome; the phrase is placed first for emphasis. |
| ru-03-0012 | B1a | history; Instrumental singular feminine. Интересоваться takes the instrumental for the subject of sustained interest. |
| ru-03-0013 | B1a | prices; Prepositional plural after о. Спрашивать о introduces the topic of a question; на картины specifies what is priced. |
| ru-03-0014 | B1a | photography; Instrumental singular feminine. Увлекаться takes the instrumental for an activity that strongly interests someone. |
| ru-03-0015 | B1a | science; Dative singular feminine. Посвящён takes the dative for a subject to which a publication is devoted. |
| ru-03-0016 | B1a | quality; Instrumental singular neuter. Довольны takes the instrumental for what satisfies the speakers. |
| ru-03-0017 | B1a | service; Instrumental singular neuter. Недовольны takes the instrumental for the cause of dissatisfaction. |
| ru-03-0018 | B1a | achievements; Instrumental plural. Гордиться takes the instrumental for the person or achievement that inspires pride. |
| ru-03-0019 | B1a | arrival; Dative singular masculine; твоему agrees with it. Рады takes the dative for an event that makes people glad. |
| ru-03-0020 | B1a | sun; Dative singular neuter. Обрадоваться takes the dative for what prompts a moment of joy. |
| ru-03-0021 | B1a | weather; Genitive singular feminine after от. Зависеть от introduces a condition that determines the outcome. |
| ru-03-0022 | B1a | parents; Genitive plural animate after от. Зависеть от can express reliance on people; финансово specifies financial dependence. |
| ru-03-0023 | B1a | consent; Genitive singular neuter. Добиться takes the genitive for the result achieved; here it is the parents' consent to the trip. |
| ru-03-0024 | B1a | data; Prepositional plural after в; дополнительных agrees with it. Нуждаться в takes the prepositional case for what is needed. |
| ru-03-0025 | B1a | advice; Dative singular masculine after благодаря. Благодаря introduces a contributing cause associated here with a favorable outcome. |
| ru-03-0026 | B1a | mistake; Genitive singular feminine after из-за. Из-за introduces the cause of the delay. |
| ru-03-0027 | B1a | forecast; Dative singular masculine inanimate. Вопреки takes the dative and contrasts the actual weather with the forecast. |
| ru-03-0028 | B1a | circumstances; Dative plural after по; семейным agrees with it. По семейным обстоятельствам gives family circumstances as a reason without specifying them. |
| ru-03-0029 | B1a | rain; Accusative singular masculine inanimate after несмотря на. Несмотря на introduces a circumstance that did not prevent the activity. |
| ru-03-0030 | B1a | permission; Genitive singular neuter after без. Без разрешения states a missing condition under which the action is prohibited. |
| ru-03-0031 | B1a | visitors; Genitive plural animate after для. Для identifies the group affected by the restriction; без билета narrows that group. |
| ru-03-0032 | B1a | patience; Genitive singular neuter. Требовать takes the genitive here for a quality needed to do the work. |
| ru-03-0033 | B1a | date; Accusative singular feminine after на. Обратить внимание на directs attention to a particular detail. |
| ru-03-0034 | B1a | house; Prepositional singular masculine after о. Мечтать о introduces a desired possibility or object. |
| ru-03-0035 | B1a | questions; Genitive plural; сложных agrees with it. Бояться takes the genitive for what someone fears. |
| ru-03-0036 | B1a | noise; Dative singular masculine after к. Привыкнуть к introduces something one has become accustomed to. |
| ru-03-0037 | B1a | sample; Instrumental singular masculine inanimate. Сравнить что-то с чем-то uses с and the instrumental for the item used as a comparison. |

| ru-03-0040 | B1a | patient; Dative singular masculine animate. Запретить takes the dative for the person subject to the prohibition and the accusative for the prohibited activity. |

| ru-03-0043 | B1a | installation; Instrumental singular feminine after с. Помочь с чем identifies the task in which someone helps. |

| ru-03-0045 | B1a | filling out; Prepositional singular neuter after при. При marks the activity during which the check should be made. |

| ru-03-0059 | B1a | pupils; Instrumental plural animate. Гордиться кем identifies the people one is proud of. |

| ru-03-0061 | B1a | delay; Instrumental singular feminine. Недовольны чем identifies the cause of dissatisfaction. |

| ru-03-0067 | B1a | help; Dative singular feminine after благодаря. Благодаря introduces a factor that contributed to the favorable result. |

| ru-03-0087 | B1a | architecture; Instrumental singular feminine; местной agrees with it. Интересоваться governs the instrumental of the subject of interest. |

| ru-03-0089 | B1a | funding; Genitive singular neuter after от. Зависеть от expresses dependence on a condition. |

| ru-03-0092 | B1a | training sessions; Dative plural feminine after благодаря. Благодаря introduces a cause credited with a positive result. |

| ru-03-0095 | B1a | renovation; Genitive singular masculine after во время. Во время places the action within the period of renovation. |
| ru-03-0096 | B1a | move to a new home; Prepositional singular masculine after при. При introduces the activity during which the cup was broken. |
| ru-03-0097 | B1a | friendship; Instrumental singular feminine; нашей agrees with дружбой. Дорожить governs the instrumental and expresses that someone values a relationship. |

| ru-03-0100 | B1a | vehicles; Genitive plural feminine after для; грузовых agrees with it. Для marks the vehicles to which the restriction applies; только limits its scope. |
| ru-03-0101 | B1b | printed out; Feminine singular past, agreeing with секретарь. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner распечатывать. Finite form распечатает; governs an accusative object. Here printing is completed before the handover. |
| ru-03-0102 | B1b | was looking; Masculine singular past, agreeing with пассажир. Imperfective; contextual perfective partner посмотреть. Finite form смотрит; смотреть на governs the accusative. The action was in progress alongside the search. |
| ru-03-0103 | B1b | was recording; Past singular; masculine записывал or feminine записывала according to the engineer’s gender, which the sentence leaves open. Imperfective; perfective partner записать. Finite form записывает; governs an accusative object. The process was underway when the lights went out. |
| ru-03-0104 | B1b | forgot; Feminine singular past; the visible form вышла identifies the speaker as female. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner забывать. Finite form забудет; governs an accusative object. It reports an earlier completed omission that explains leaving home without the pass. |
| ru-03-0105 | B1b | wrote regularly; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Антон. Imperfective; contextual perfective partner написать. Finite form пишет; governs an accusative object. Каждый вечер marks repeated events. |
| ru-03-0106 | B1b | asked for; Plural past, agreeing with мы. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner просить. Finite form попросит; governs an accusative object here. The request precedes the choice of route. |
| ru-03-0107 | B1b | send; Perfective infinitive after хотела. Perfective; imperfective partner отправлять. Finite form отправит; governs an accusative object. The infinitive expresses the intended completed act, without asserting that it happened. |
| ru-03-0108 | B1b | open; Perfective infinitive after пыталась. Perfective; imperfective partner открывать. Finite form откроет; governs an accusative object. The attempted result is not achieved in this context. |
| ru-03-0109 | B1b | closed; Feminine singular past in the canonical answer; masculine закрыл is also accepted because the speaker’s gender is not established. Заперта agrees with дверь. Perfective; imperfective partner закрывать. Finite form закроет; governs an accusative object. The completed action produces the stated present result. |
| ru-03-0110 | B1b | was setting; Masculine singular past, agreeing with хозяин. Imperfective; perfective partner накрыть. Finite form накрывает; накрывать стол takes an accusative object. Ещё presents the work as unfinished at the visitors' arrival. |
| ru-03-0111 | B1b | clarified; Masculine singular past, agreeing with начальник. Imperfective; perfective partner уточнить. Finite form уточняет; governs an accusative object. Каждый раз signals a repeated action. |
| ru-03-0112 | B1b | stopped; Masculine singular reflexive past, agreeing with поезд. Perfective; imperfective partner останавливаться. Finite form остановится; intransitive here. The completed stop precedes the passengers' exit. |
| ru-03-0113 | B1b | visit; Perfective infinitive after собирались. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner посещать. Finite form посетит; governs an accusative object. The intended visit did not take place. |
| ru-03-0114 | B1b | was calming down; Masculine singular reflexive past, agreeing with ребёнок. Imperfective; perfective partner успокоиться. Finite form успокаивается; intransitive. The gradual process runs alongside the wait. |
| ru-03-0115 | B1b | tightened; Past singular; masculine затянул or feminine затянула according to the repair worker’s gender, which the sentence leaves open. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner затягивать. Finite form затянет; governs an accusative object. The completed tightening explains the present stable state. |
| ru-03-0116 | B1b | changed; Plural past, agreeing with мы. Imperfective; contextual perfective partner сменить. Finite form меняет; governs an accusative object. Несколько раз marks repeated changes during one trip. |
| ru-03-0117 | B1b | fill in; Imperfective infinitive after начала. Imperfective; perfective partner заполнить. Finite form заполняет; governs an accusative object. Начала introduces the process, which the call interrupts. |
| ru-03-0118 | B1b | did not send; Negated masculine singular past, agreeing with Андрей. Perfective; imperfective partner отправлять. Finite form отправит; governs an accusative object. Negation states that the intended completed sending did not occur. |
| ru-03-0119 | B1b | corrected; Feminine singular past; нашла identifies бухгалтер as female. Perfective; imperfective partner исправлять. Finite form исправит; governs an accusative object. The correction follows the discovery in the narrative sequence. |
| ru-03-0120 | B1b | were packing; Plural past, agreeing with соседи. Imperfective; perfective partner упаковать. Finite form упаковывают; governs an accusative object. Весь вечер presents packing as a process over the evening. |
| ru-03-0121 | B1b | handed over; Masculine singular past, agreeing with директор. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner передавать. Finite form: передаст. Takes an accusative object and a dative recipient; the handover follows the signing. |
| ru-03-0122 | B1b | was telling; Masculine singular past in the canonical answer; feminine рассказывала is also accepted because экскурсовод may refer to a woman. Imperfective; contextual perfective partner рассказать. Finite form: рассказывает. Here о takes the prepositional case; the ongoing account provides the background for the photographs. |
| ru-03-0123 | B1b | used to inspect; Masculine singular past in the canonical answer; feminine осматривала is also accepted because водитель may refer to a woman. Imperfective; contextual perfective partner осмотреть. Finite form: осматривает. Takes an accusative object; каждое утро marks a repeated action. |
| ru-03-0124 | B1b | order; Perfective infinitive after собирались. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner заказывать. Finite form: закажем. Takes an accusative object; the infinitive expresses an intended single order, not an accomplished one. |
| ru-03-0125 | B1b | lift; Perfective infinitive after пыталась. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner поднимать. Finite form: поднимет. Takes an accusative object; the infinitive names the intended result of an unsuccessful attempt. |
| ru-03-0126 | B1b | turned off; Masculine singular past in the canonical answer; feminine перекрыла is also accepted because мастер may refer to a woman. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner перекрывать. Finite form: перекро́ет. Takes an accusative object; the completed shutoff explains the present lack of running water. |
| ru-03-0127 | B1b | boarded; Plural past, agreeing with мы. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner садиться. Finite form: сядем. В takes the accusative for boarding; the completed action follows the ticket purchase. |
| ru-03-0128 | B1b | was typing; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Ирина. Imperfective; contextual perfective partner напечатать. Finite form: печатает. Takes an accusative object; the typing was in progress when the lights went out. |
| ru-03-0129 | B1b | used to write down; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Павел. Imperfective; contextual perfective partner записать. Finite form: записывает. Takes an accusative object; часто marks repeated note-taking. |
| ru-03-0130 | B1b | paid; Plural past, agreeing with гости. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner платить. Finite form: заплатят. Used without an object here; the completed payment follows receipt of the bill. |
| ru-03-0131 | B1b | collect; Perfective infinitive after хотела. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner забирать. Finite form: заберёт. Takes an accusative object; the infinitive expresses an intended completed collection. |
| ru-03-0132 | B1b | discuss; Perfective infinitive after не успели. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner обсуждать. Finite form: обсудим. Takes an accusative object; не успели places the intended completed discussion beyond the available time. |
| ru-03-0133 | B1b | lost; Feminine singular past in the canonical answer; masculine потерял is also accepted because the speaker’s gender is not visible outside the cloze. Perfective; contextual imperfective partner терять. Finite form: потеряет. Takes an accusative object; the completed loss explains the present difficulty. |
| ru-03-0134 | B1b | were talking; Plural past, agreeing with родители. Imperfective; contextual perfective partner поговорить denotes a bounded conversation. Finite form: разговаривают. Used without an object here; the conversation overlaps with the children's sleep. |

Ездить has no simple perfective aspect partner for this repeated-travel use. |
| ru-03-0695 | B1g | would have seemed; Feminine singular reflexive past form with бы agrees with дорога. Perfective; imperfective partner казаться. Покажется, покажутся; takes the predicative comparative короче. |
| ru-03-0696 | B1g | would not have spoiled; Negated masculine singular reflexive past form with бы agrees with сыр. Perfective; imperfective partner портиться. Испортится, испортятся; intransitive, expressing an unreal past result. |
| ru-03-0697 | B1g | would give up; Feminine singular past form with visible бы expresses willingness; самой identifies the same speaker’s gender. Perfective; imperfective partner уступать. Уступлю, уступят; takes dative вам and accusative место. |
| ru-03-0698 | B1g | would have reread; Feminine singular past form with бы agrees with Лена. Perfective; imperfective partner перечитывать. Перечитаю, перечитают; takes the accusative письмо. The prefix expresses reading again. |
| ru-03-0699 | B1g | would compare; Masculine singular past form with visible бы gives advice; сравнила is the feminine alternative. Perfective; imperfective partner сравнивать. Сравню, сравнят; takes the accusative цены. |
| ru-03-0700 | B1g | would have accompanied; Feminine singular past form with visible бы expresses an unreal past action; проводил is the masculine alternative. Perfective; imperfective partner провожать. Провожу, проводят; takes animate accusative тебя and до with the genitive destination. |
| ru-03-0701 | B1h | wrote; Feminine singular past in the canonical answer; masculine написал is also accepted because коллега does not independently establish gender. Perfective; imperfective partner писать. Напишу, напишут; introduces the message content with что. The subordinate future пришлёт reports the colleague’s intended future action. |
| ru-03-0702 | B1h | reported; Feminine singular past form agrees with Анна. Perfective; imperfective partner сообщать. Сообщу, сообщат; что introduces the information reported. Её refers to Анна; the future прибудет preserves the reported arrival as future. |
| ru-03-0703 | B1h | asked; Masculine singular past form agrees with Дима. Perfective; imperfective partner спрашивать. Спрошу, спросят; introduces an indirect yes-or-no question. Ли follows готов and marks the embedded question. |
| ru-03-0704 | B1h | asked; Feminine singular past form agrees with Оля. Perfective; imperfective partner просить. Попрошу, попросят; takes the animate accusative меня and infinitive закрыть. The infinitive reports the action requested from меня. |
| ru-03-0705 | B1h | it is stated; Short neuter passive form used impersonally to attribute the statement to the announcement. Сказать is perfective; imperfective partner говорить. Скажу, скажут; здесь что introduces the stated content. В объявлении names the written source; сказано does not name an individual author. |
| ru-03-0706 | B1h | according to; По governs the dative plural словам; the source Иры is genitive. The phrase attributes the information to Ира; её брат is her brother. |
| ru-03-0707 | B1h | asked for clarification; Masculine singular past form agrees with Максим. Perfective; imperfective partner уточнять. Уточню, уточнят; here followed by an indirect question introduced by во сколько. |
| ru-03-0708 | B1h | warned; Feminine singular past form agrees with Анна. Perfective; imperfective partner предупреждать. Предупрежу, предупредят; takes animate accusative пациента and a что clause stating the warning. |
| ru-03-0709 | B1h | passed on the message; Masculine singular past in the canonical answer; feminine передала is also accepted because Саша is a gender-ambiguous name here. Perfective; imperfective partner передавать. Передам, передадут; что introduces the message. The subject of задержится is understood as Саша. |
| ru-03-0710 | B1h | reminded; Feminine singular past form agrees with Нина. Perfective; imperfective partner напоминать. Напомню, напомнят; takes dative нам and a что clause giving the reminder. |
| ru-03-0711 | B1h | explained; Masculine singular past form agrees with Пётр. Perfective; imperfective partner объяснять. Объясню, объяснят; takes dative детям and an indirect question introduced by почему. |
| ru-03-0712 | B1h | suggested; Feminine singular past form agrees with Марина. Perfective; imperfective partner предлагать. Предложу, предложат; чтобы introduces the proposed action by мы. Встретились is a past-shaped form in the proposed-action clause, not a report that the meeting occurred. |
| ru-03-0713 | B1h | it is specified; Short neuter passive form used impersonally; the site is the source. Указать is perfective; imperfective partner указывать. Укажу, укажут; когда introduces the information specified. |
| ru-03-0714 | B1h | told; Feminine singular past form agrees with подруга. Perfective; imperfective partner рассказывать. Расскажу, расскажут; takes dative мне and a как clause describing the events. Она refers back to подруга. |
| ru-03-0715 | B1h | replied; Masculine singular past form agrees with Андрей. Perfective; imperfective partner отвечать. Отвечу, ответят; что introduces the content of the reply. Получил refers to Андрей; пока не presents receipt as still pending. |
| ru-03-0716 | B1h | requested; Feminine singular past form agrees with учительница. Perfective; imperfective partner просить. Попрошу, попросят; чтобы introduces the requested behavior by дети. Не шумели is past-shaped but expresses the content of the request, not a completed past event. |
| ru-03-0717 | B1h | announced; Plural past form with an unspecified human subject reports an announcement. Perfective; imperfective partner объявлять. Объявлю, объявят; что introduces the announced information. По радио identifies the medium; откроют also leaves the people responsible unspecified. |
| ru-03-0718 | B1h | asked; Feminine singular reflexive past form agrees with Ирина. Perfective; imperfective partner интересоваться. Поинтересуюсь, поинтересуются; here followed by an indirect yes-or-no question. Не нужен ли makes the enquiry tentative; её refers to Ирина. |
| ru-03-0719 | B1h | it says; Third-person singular present reflexive form used impersonally. Imperfective; no simple aspect partner in this impersonal source-attribution use. Second conjugation; impersonal говорится, говорилось; что introduces the reported content. В заметке identifies the written source. |
| ru-03-0720 | B1h | admitted; Masculine singular reflexive past form agrees with Борис. Perfective; imperfective partner признаваться. Признаюсь, признаются; takes dative сестре and a что clause stating what he admitted. Её refers to the sister; забыл refers to Борис. |
| ru-03-0721 | B1h | assured; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Лена. Perfective; imperfective partner заверять. Present-future forms include заверю and заверит. Заверить кого-то, что introduces an assurance; the clause reports a future action from Lena's perspective. The accusative нас identifies the people receiving the assurance. |
| ru-03-0722 | B1h | promised; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Денис. Perfective; imperfective partner обещать. Present-future forms include пообещаю and пообещает. Пообещать кому-то, что introduces the promised action, which is future relative to the promise. The dative сестре identifies the person to whom Denis made the promise. |
| ru-03-0723 | B1h | confirmed; Masculine singular past; feminine подтвердила is also possible because директор can refer to a woman. Perfective; imperfective partner подтверждать. Present-future forms include подтвержу and подтвердит. Подтвердить, что introduces the fact being confirmed; состоится is future relative to the confirmation. |
| ru-03-0724 | B1h | added; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Вика. Perfective; imperfective partner добавлять. Present-future forms include добавлю and добавит. Добавить, что introduces an additional reported statement; сможет refers to a future possibility. Here добавить means to add information to what has been said. |
| ru-03-0725 | B1h | objected; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Роман. Perfective; imperfective partner возражать. Present-future forms include возражу and возразит. Возразить, что introduces the content of an objection; нужно presents the need as current in the report. |
| ru-03-0726 | B1h | insisted; Feminine singular past, agreeing with мама. Perfective; imperfective partner настаивать. Future forms include настою and настоит. Настоять, чтобы introduces the action insisted upon; the clause does not itself establish that the action happened. Чтобы мы вызвали врача reports what the mother insisted should be done. |
| ru-03-0727 | B1h | recommended; Masculine singular past; feminine рекомендовала is also possible because врач can refer to a woman. Рекомендовать can be imperfective or perfective; here it reports a recommendation as a whole. Finite forms include рекомендую and рекомендует. Рекомендовать кому-то with an infinitive names the advised action. The dative Игорю identifies the recipient; imperfective оставаться presents the advised state over several days. |
| ru-03-0728 | B1h | allowed; Masculine singular past; feminine разрешила is also possible because учитель can refer to a woman. Perfective; imperfective partner разрешать. Present-future forms include разрешу and разрешит. Разрешить кому-то with an infinitive gives permission for an action. The dative детям identifies the people given permission; пользоваться describes the activity allowed during the task. |
| ru-03-0729 | B1h | forbade; Masculine singular past, agreeing with папа. Perfective; imperfective partner запрещать. Present-future forms include запрещу and запретит. Запретить кому-то with an infinitive reports a prohibition. The dative детям names the people subject to the prohibition; imperfective открывать describes the prohibited behavior. |
| ru-03-0730 | B1h | that; Introduces a clause with the past form начали to report a proposed action, not an action established as completed. After предложил, чтобы introduces a suggestion involving the subject мы. |
| ru-03-0731 | B1h | she; Nominative singular feminine; subject of прислала. Она refers back to Наташу and identifies the person asked to send the address. |
| ru-03-0732 | B1h | her; Possessive form; it does not change to agree with брат. Её refers to Оля and identifies the brother as hers. |
| ru-03-0733 | B1h | his own; Accusative singular masculine inanimate, agreeing with пропуск. Свой refers to the subject of забыл: the pass belongs to Сергей. |
| ru-03-0734 | B1h | tomorrow; Сегодня places Anton's statement on the current day, so завтра refers to the day after the current day. |
| ru-03-0735 | B1h | the next day; На governs the accusative phrase следующий день. The phrase places the sending on the day after Lisa's statement, whose time is given as yesterday. |
| ru-03-0736 | B1h | noted; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Нина. Perfective; imperfective partner отмечать. Present-future forms include отмечу and отметит. Отметить, что introduces a fact singled out in speech; стали places the change before the report. |
| ru-03-0737 | B1h | remarked; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Катя. Perfective; imperfective замечать. Second conjugation: замечу, заметит. | ru-03-0738 | B1h | complained; Masculine singular reflexive past, agreeing with Миша. Perfective; imperfective partner жаловаться. Present-future forms include пожалуюсь and пожалуется. Пожаловаться, что introduces the content of a complaint; слушали describes the extended past activity. |
| ru-03-0739 | B1h | mentioned; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Олег. Perfective; imperfective partner упоминать. Future forms include упомяну and упомянет. | ru-03-0740 | B1h | insists; Third-person singular present, agreeing with соседка. Imperfective; perfective partner уверить. Present forms include уверяю and уверяет. Уверять, что introduces a statement the source asserts confidently. The present form attributes the claim to the neighbor without establishing that it is true. |
| ru-03-0741 | B1h | citing; С governs instrumental singular ссылкой; на governs accusative музей. The phrase attributes the newspaper's information to the museum as its source. |
| ru-03-0742 | B1h | from the announcement; Из governs the genitive singular объявления. The phrase names the written source from which the speakers learned the information. |
| ru-03-0743 | B1h | according to the timetable; Согласно governs the dative singular расписанию. The phrase attributes the departure time to the timetable. |
| ru-03-0744 | B1h | forecast; Third-person plural present with an unspecified human subject. Imperfective; perfective partner пообещать. Present forms include обещаю and обещают. | ru-03-0745 | B1h | if one believes; Если introduces a condition; верить is an infinitive governing the dative прогнозу. Верить is imperfective, with no simple aspectual partner in this use. Present forms include верю and верит. Верить кому-либо or чему-либо takes the dative; the infinitive here presents reliance on the forecast as a condition. The phrase qualifies the prediction as dependent on the forecast's reliability. |
| ru-03-0746 | B1h | it is written; Short neuter passive participle used impersonally. Написать is perfective; its imperfective partner is писать. Future forms include напишу and напишет. Написать can take an accusative object or an indirect question giving the written content. Написано presents the information as available in writing without asserting a new completed act. The indirect question gives the destination for sending the form. |
| ru-03-0747 | B1h | whether it is possible; Ли follows the impersonal predicate можно; the infinitive оплатить names the action. The construction introduces an indirect yes-or-no question about the availability of card payment. |
| ru-03-0748 | B1h | whether it is ready; Готов is a short masculine singular adjective agreeing with доклад; ли follows it. The construction introduces an indirect yes-or-no question about the report's readiness. |
| ru-03-0749 | B1h | whether it will be possible; Third-person singular future; ли follows the finite verb. Perfective; imperfective partner мочь. Future forms include смогу and сможет. | ru-03-0750 | B1h | which of the two; Какой is an accusative singular masculine inanimate pronoun standing for the selected вариант; из governs the genitive двух вариантов. The colleague asks which of the two stated options the speakers chose. |
| ru-03-0751 | B1h | what on; Instrumental form чем follows над. Работать над чем-то names the project or task being worked on; the phrase introduces an indirect question. |
| ru-03-0752 | B1h | for whom; Genitive form кого follows для. Полезен для кого-то identifies the people who would benefit; для кого introduces the indirect question. |
| ru-03-0753 | B1h | until what date; До governs genitive singular какого числа. The phrase asks for the calendar date marking the end of the permitted stay. |
| ru-03-0754 | B1h | told; Feminine singular past, agreeing with мама. Велеть is biaspectual in dictionaries; this past form presents a completed instruction. Second conjugation: велю, велит. The dative сыну names the person instructed; выключить presents switching off as a completed action. |
| ru-03-0755 | B1h | not to make noise; Negative infinitive; не precedes the verb. Imperfective; пошуметь means to make noise for a while rather than serving as a general aspect partner. | ru-03-0756 | B1h | he; Nominative singular masculine; subject of взял. Тот refers back to сыну and makes the recipient of the reminder the person who should take the keys. |
| ru-03-0757 | B1h | according to data from; По governs the dative plural данным; музея is genitive. The phrase attributes the attendance figure to the museum's data. |
| ru-03-0758 | B1h | learned; Plural past, agreeing with мы. Perfective; imperfective partner узнавать. Future forms include узнаю and узнает. Узнать о чём-то reports acquiring information; из новостей names the source. О закрытии identifies the event the speakers learned about. |
| ru-03-0759 | B1h | had already sent; Feminine singular past, agreeing with the understood subject Ира; уже marks prior completion. Perfective; imperfective partner отправлять. | ru-03-0760 | B1h | at that time; In Dima's later report, тогда refers back to the cancellation in May; ещё не знал describes what he did not yet know at that time. |
| ru-03-0761 | B1h | whether one has forgotten; Masculine singular past with не and interrogative ли in the canonical answer; feminine не забыла ли is also accepted because the speaker’s gender is not visible. Забыть is perfective; the contextual imperfective partner is забывать. Забыть takes an accusative object; forms include забуду and забудет. The past reports a possible completed act. Не забыл ли introduces a polite indirect yes-or-no question; the negative form does not establish that the keys were forgotten. |
| ru-03-0762 | B1h | whether one will; Third-person singular future auxiliary followed by ли; она is the subject. Быть is imperfective, with no simple aspectual partner in this auxiliary use. The construction reports a yes-or-no question about Marina's future plans. |
| ru-03-0763 | B1h | whether received; Masculine singular past with ли; он refers to Игорь. Получить is perfective; its contextual imperfective partner is получать. It takes an accusative object; forms include получу and получит. The past presents receipt as a completed event. |
| ru-03-0764 | B1h | whether knows; Third-person singular present with ли; она refers to Катя. Знать is imperfective with no simple aspectual partner for this state. It takes an accusative object; forms include знаю and знают. The present reports a state rather than a completed event. |
| ru-03-0765 | B1h | whether everyone; Nominative plural pronoun followed by ли; все is the subject of получили. Ли follows все, focusing the indirect question on whether the statement applies to every person. |
| ru-03-0766 | B1h | whether often; The interrogative particle ли follows the adverb часто. The question concerns the frequency of Anton's headaches. |
| ru-03-0767 | B1h | whether for long; The interrogative particle ли follows the adverb долго. The indirect question asks whether the wait lasted a long time. |
| ru-03-0768 | B1h | whether herself; Nominative singular feminine, agreeing with она; ли follows сама. Сама ли focuses the question on whether Pavel's sister baked the pie herself. |
| ru-03-0769 | B1h | whether to her; Dative singular feminine pronoun followed by ли; ей is the person who needs the dictionary. Ли focuses the indirect question on whether Nina, rather than someone else, needs it. |
| ru-03-0770 | B1h | from which floor; С governs the genitive singular phrase какого этажа. The phrase introduces an indirect question about the viewpoint from which the river is visible. |
| ru-03-0771 | B1h | for how much; За is followed by the interrogative quantity word сколько. With купила, за сколько asks the price paid for the bicycle. |
| ru-03-0772 | B1h | in how many days; Сколько governs the genitive plural дней after через. The phrase asks about the interval before the brother's return. |
| ru-03-0773 | B1h | what for; Для governs the genitive form чего. Для чего introduces an indirect question about the button's purpose. |
| ru-03-0774 | B1h | without what; Без governs the genitive form чего. The indirect question asks which ingredient is essential for the soup. |
| ru-03-0775 | B1h | on what; На governs the accusative form что. Потратить деньги на что asks what the money was spent on. |
| ru-03-0776 | B1h | with which doctor; К governs the dative singular phrase какому врачу. Записаться к врачу means to make an appointment with a doctor. |
| ru-03-0777 | B1h | after which lesson; После governs the genitive singular phrase какого урока. The phrase asks which lesson marks the start of the brother's free time. |
| ru-03-0778 | B1h | which of the guests; Кого is the animate accusative object of пригласила; из governs genitive plural гостей. The question asks which guest Anna invited first. |
| ru-03-0779 | B1h | which film; Nominative singular masculine phrase; какой agrees with фильм, the subject of понравился. The question asks which film Olya's friend liked better. |
| ru-03-0780 | B1h | whose phone; Instrumental singular masculine; чьим agrees with телефоном. Воспользоваться governs the instrumental and means to use something on a particular occasion. |
| ru-03-0781 | B1h | dissuaded; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Нина. Отговорить is perfective; its contextual imperfective partner is отговаривать. Future forms include отговорю and отговорит. | ru-03-0782 | B1h | not to touch; Negative imperfective infinitive following попросил. Трогать is imperfective; the contextual perfective partner is тронуть. Forms include трогаю and трогает. | ru-03-0783 | B1h | bring; Plural past form, agreeing with дети; after чтобы it expresses a requested action. Принести is perfective; its contextual imperfective partner is приносить. Future forms include принесу and принесут. It takes an accusative object and presents the requested delivery as a complete act. The prefix при- marks arrival with the notebooks; the base нести is unidirectional, unlike multidirectional носить. The means of travel are unspecified. After попросила, чтобы, the form reports a request and does not establish that the children brought the notebooks. |
| ru-03-0784 | B1h | persuaded; Masculine singular past, agreeing with Иван. Уговорить is perfective; its contextual imperfective partner is уговаривать. Forms include уговорю and уговорит. It takes an accusative person and an infinitive; the perfective reports successful persuasion. |
| ru-03-0785 | B1h | they; Nominative plural personal pronoun; subject of помогли. Они refers to the plural addressee group детей, distinguishing them from the singular reporting source мама. |
| ru-03-0786 | B1h | will help her; Third-person singular future verb with a dative singular feminine pronoun; the subject is Антон and ей refers to сестре. Помочь is perfective; its contextual imperfective partner is помогать. Forms include помогу and поможет. It governs the dative person; the future reports the action Anton promised. |
| ru-03-0787 | B1h | today; Сегодня is anchored to the narrator’s explicitly stated present Tuesday. It does not reproduce a supposed direct quotation spoken on Monday; the reported day off is Tuesday. |
| ru-03-0788 | B1h | yesterday; Сегодня утром anchors Lena's report to the current day; вчера identifies the preceding day. |
| ru-03-0789 | B1h | two days later; Через introduces a duration; два is followed by genitive singular дня. The interval is anchored to Monday, the day of Dima's warning. |
| ru-03-0790 | B1h | a year before that; За governs accusative singular год to express the interval; до governs genitive того. The interval is measured back from Olya's report in January. |
| ru-03-0791 | B1h | a month earlier; Instrumental singular месяцем expresses the difference in time before comparative раньше. The move took place one month before Nina's June report. |
| ru-03-0792 | B1h | the day before; Накануне refers to Friday, the day before Igor's Saturday message. |
| ru-03-0793 | B1h | from the letter; Из governs genitive singular письма; Анны is a genitive name associating Anna with the letter. The phrase identifies the written source from which the speaker learned Anna's location. |
| ru-03-0794 | B1h | as told; Feminine singular past agrees with Оля; мне is the dative recipient. Рассказать is perfective; its contextual imperfective partner is рассказывать. Forms include расскажу and расскажет. It can take a dative listener and a report of the information; the past presents one report. Как introduces Olya as the source; её брат refers to Olya's brother. |
| ru-03-0795 | B1h | from the neighbor; От governs genitive singular соседки. Слышать от кого identifies the person who supplied the information. |
| ru-03-0796 | B1h | clarified; Plural past with an unspecified human subject. Уточнить is perfective; its contextual imperfective partner is уточнять. Forms include уточню and уточнят. Here it introduces a что-clause giving a clarification; the past reports one clarification. The library website is the medium through which the clarification was published. |
| ru-03-0797 | B1h | apologized; Feminine singular reflexive past, agreeing with администрация. Извиниться is perfective; its contextual imperfective partner is извиняться. Forms include извинюсь and извинится. It takes за with the accusative for the reason; the past reports one apology. |
| ru-03-0798 | B1h | their; Invariable third-person plural possessive form modifying родители. Их refers to Nina and her brother; the parents are the topic of her message. |
| ru-03-0799 | B1h | she; Nominative singular feminine; subject of может взять. Та refers to Катя, the addressee, identifying the person allowed to take the umbrella. |
| ru-03-0800 | B1h | as it turned out; Выяснилось is a neuter singular past form used impersonally in the introductory expression. Выясниться is perfective; imperfective выясняться. Finite forms include выяснится and выясняется. The fact can be given in a content clause, while из plus genitive identifies its source; here из письма names the source. Как выяснилось introduces a fact that became known. It is a fixed introductory expression, not a converb construction. |
| ru-03-0801 | B1i | who; Nominative singular masculine, agreeing with сосед; subject of живёт. |

| ru-03-1088 | B1k | without noticing; Negated imperfective adverbial participle; мы remained unaware of the time during the discussion. Замечать is imperfective; the perfective partner is заметить. Compare замеча́ю, замеча́ют; negated замечать can take a genitive object, here времени. Controlled productive written use: мы is the shared subject. |
| ru-03-1089 | B1k | having solved; Perfective adverbial participle; Маша solved the problem before explaining her answer. Решить is perfective; the imperfective partner is решать. Compare решу́, реша́т; it takes an accusative object and its finite present-shaped forms refer to the future. Controlled productive written use: Маша is the shared subject. |
| ru-03-1090 | B1k | having saved; Perfective adverbial participle; я saved the file before sending it. Сохранить is perfective; the imperfective partner is сохранять. Compare сохраню́, сохраня́т; it takes an accusative object and its finite present-shaped forms refer to the future. Controlled productive written use: я is the shared subject. |
| ru-03-1091 | B1k | on noticing; Perfective adverbial participle; Ольга noticed the error before speaking to the cashier. Заметить is perfective; the imperfective partner is замечать. Compare заме́чу, заме́тят; it takes an accusative object and its finite present-shaped forms refer to the future. Recognition: identify Ольга as the shared subject and the noticing as prior. |
| ru-03-1092 | B1k | having gone up; Reflexive perfective adverbial participle; гости went upstairs before seeing the door. Подняться is perfective; the imperfective partner is подниматься. Compare подниму́сь, подни́мутся. The prefix под- marks upward movement to на plus accusative; the means of travel is unspecified. Recognition: гости is the shared subject. |
| ru-03-1093 | B1k | having rested; Perfective adverbial participle; туристы rested before setting out. Отдохнуть is perfective; the imperfective partner is отдыхать. Compare отдохну́, отдохну́т; it is intransitive and its finite present-shaped forms refer to the future. Recognition: туристы is the shared subject. |
| ru-03-1094 | B1k | having called; Perfective adverbial participle; Ирина made the call before booking the appointment. Позвонить is perfective; the imperfective partner is звонить. Compare позвоню́, позвоня́т; в plus accusative names the institution called, and finite present-shaped forms refer to the future. Recognition: Ирина is the shared subject. |
| ru-03-1095 | B1k | having laid out; Perfective adverbial participle; юрист laid out the documents before studying them. Разложить is perfective; the imperfective partner is раскладывать. Compare разложу́, разло́жат; it takes an accusative object and its finite present-shaped forms refer to the future. Recognition: юрист is the shared subject. |
| ru-03-1096 | B1k | without confirming; Negated perfective adverbial participle; мы had not confirmed the time before arriving. Уточнить is perfective; the imperfective partner is уточнять. Compare уточню́, уточня́т; it takes an accusative object and its finite present-shaped forms refer to the future. Recognition: мы is the shared subject. |
| ru-03-1097 | B1k | without taking; Negated perfective adverbial participle; покупатель had left without taking the receipt. Взять is perfective; the imperfective partner is брать. Compare возьму́, возьму́т; it takes an accusative object and its finite present-shaped forms refer to the future. Recognition: покупатель is the shared subject; negation expresses an omitted prior action. |
| ru-03-1098 | B1k | while holding; Imperfective, non-reflexive adverbial participle in -а. Анна held the cup while she opened the door. Держать is imperfective and governs the accusative чашку. There is no simple perfective partner for this ongoing holding sense. Compare держу́ and де́ржит. Controlled productive written use: the participle describes an action simultaneous with the main action and shares its subject. |
| ru-03-1099 | B1k | while leaning; Imperfective reflexive adverbial participle in -ясь. Дедушка leaned on the railing as he climbed the stairs. Опираться is imperfective; опереться is its perfective partner for taking support. Compare опира́юсь and опира́ется. The verb governs на with the accusative, here перила. Controlled productive written use: the participle describes support during the main action and shares its subject. |
| ru-03-1100 | B1k | without interrupting; Negated imperfective adverbial participle; Таня did not interrupt during the story. Перебивать is imperfective; the perfective partner is перебить. Compare перебива́ю, перебива́ют; it can take an accusative person as its object. Recognition: Таня is the shared subject of listening and not interrupting. |
