| ru-03-1266 | B1m | even; Даже marks asking for another helping as strong evidence that the speaker liked the soup. |
| ru-03-1267 | B1m | only; Только restricts the people who have finished to Лена. |
| ru-03-1268 | B1m | only; Только limits what the speaker has done to opening the letter. |
| ru-03-1269 | B1m | not until; Только restricts the possible arrival time to after lunch. |
| ru-03-1270 | B1m | what a difference; То ли дело introduces an approving contrast with the noisy place mentioned first. |
| ru-03-1271 | B1m | but; Только introduces a condition on the accepted offer. |
| ru-03-1272 | B1m | only; Только restricts the kind of tea the speaker drinks to green tea. |
| ru-03-1273 | B1m | grandmother; Nominative singular feminine; subject of испекла. The subject follows the verb as the answer to кто. |
| ru-03-1274 | B1m | very much indeed; An emphatic affirmative answer to the question about enjoying the concert. |
| ru-03-1275 | B1m | of course; The affirmative response presents the answer as obvious; the year spent waiting explains why. |
| ru-03-1276 | B1m | as if I would; A dismissive colloquial refusal of the unreasonable demand; it can sound blunt. |
| ru-03-1277 | B1m | only; Только и precedes the finite verb говорит; что introduces the topic after it. Только и ... что restricts his conversation to work and can express dissatisfaction with the habit. |
| ru-03-1278 | B1m | that's all; Minimizes the extent of the requirement just stated: confirming the address is all that is needed. |
| ru-03-1279 | B1m | since indeed; Emphasizes the already stated commitment as the premise for the consequence that follows. |
| ru-03-1280 | B1m | what is worse; Introduces a stronger problem on top of the delay: the same bus broke down. |
| ru-03-1281 | B1m | not; Не negates the accusative singular object чай; а introduces the correction. The contrast corrects the identity of the drink, while the order itself remains true. |
| ru-03-1282 | B1m | not; Invariant negative particle with scope over the finite purchase predicate; the а-clause states the different action actually performed. The contrast rejects the purchase itself and replaces it with a different action. |
| ru-03-1283 | B1m | as for; The fixed introductory construction is followed by a genitive noun. Касаться is imperfective, first conjugation. This fixed topic-introduction use has no simple perfective partner; касается is third-person singular present and governs genitive оплаты. The construction introduces a topic rather than physical contact. Introduces оплаты as the topic resumed by её in the following clause. |
| ru-03-1284 | B1m | but as for; Introduces вечером as the contrasting time for which the speaker has a different answer. |
| ru-03-1285 | B1m | myself; Nominative masculine singular emphatic pronoun; visible собрал identifies the same speaker as male. Emphasizes the speaker's own participation without another person's help. |
| ru-03-1286 | B1m | what a; Introduces an evaluative exclamation about the surprising degree of cold; the frozen river reinforces the negative reaction. |
| ru-03-1287 | B1m | as I was saying; Resumes the topic of tickets after the interruption. |
| ru-03-1288 | B1m | exactly; Highlights an exact spatial fit, rather than a fortunate response to a need. |
| ru-03-1289 | B1m | at least; Presents beginning the discussion as a modest positive result despite uncertainty. |
| ru-03-1290 | B1m | at least; Limits the positive claim to what is established despite the unresolved decision. |
| ru-03-1291 | B1m | and on top of that; Adds an action beyond what had been promised. |
| ru-03-1292 | B1m | exactly; A reply confirming the preceding interpretation: the speaker proposes moving the meeting to Friday. |
| ru-03-1293 | B1m | really; Marks disbelief at a possibility that conflicts with the speaker's expectation. |
| ru-03-1294 | B1m | well, indeed; A rueful acknowledgement of the cancellation and its unwelcome consequence. |
| ru-03-1295 | B1m | wouldn't it be better; The particle ли follows the comparative лучше; the suggested action is expressed by the infinitive поехать. Suggests the metro as a preferable choice in the icy conditions. |
| ru-03-1296 | B1m | not at all; A formal negative reply rejecting the view attributed to the speaker. |
| ru-03-1297 | B1m | almost; Fixed scalar particle combination qualifying the following extreme quantity phrase; it does not negate читают. Qualifies the extreme claim во всех странах: the speaker suggests that nearly all countries are included. |
| ru-03-1298 | B1m | more than just; Rejects an insufficient description before a stronger characterization introduced by а. |
| ru-03-1299 | B1m | and even that; Fixed limiting particle combination adding a drawback to the preceding statement. Adds a limitation to the one available room: it has no window. |
| ru-03-1300 | B1m | not that; Fixed corrective discourse construction qualifying an attributed interpretation before the но-clause states the actual obstacle. Qualifies a possible interpretation before the speaker explains the actual obstacle. |
| ru-03-1301 | B1n | feedback; Accusative singular feminine after за; обратную agrees with связь. Refers to comments or evaluation received in response to work. |
| ru-03-1302 | B1n | review; Accusative singular inanimate masculine; direct object of прислала. Отзыв о takes the prepositional case to name the subject of an opinion or review. |
| ru-03-1303 | B1n | in the attachment; Во governs prepositional singular вложении. Identifies the file attached to the message sent to the client. |
| ru-03-1304 | B1n | as a copy recipient; В governs accusative singular копию. Поставить кого-то в копию письма means to include someone as a copy recipient. |
| ru-03-1305 | B1n | acknowledged; Fixed use of the short neuter singular passive participle of принять. Принять is perfective; принимать is its imperfective partner. Принять takes an accusative object; its future forms include при́мет. Here Принято is a fixed response, not a finite first-person form. Anna acknowledges the instruction; проверю states that she will check the contract later. |
| ru-03-1306 | B1n | be delayed; First person singular future, reflexive. Задержаться is perfective; задерживаться is its imperfective partner. Finite forms include задержусь and задержишься. It takes no direct object here and refers to a future delay. A natural neutral way to tell a friend that one will be delayed. |
| ru-03-1307 | B1n | rather; Introduces a correction to the time just given. |
| ru-03-1308 | B1n | misheard; Perfective past singular reflexive form; feminine ослышалась is canonical and masculine ослышался is accepted because the speaker gender is not specified elsewhere. Ослышаться is perfective; ослышиваться is its imperfective partner for repeated mistakes. Finite forms include ослы́шусь and ослы́шишься. It takes no direct object here and reports a completed mishearing. The speaker politely repairs a misunderstanding before asking for the detail again. |
| ru-03-1309 | B1n | did not hear clearly; Negated perfective past singular; masculine расслышал is canonical and feminine расслышала is accepted because the speaker gender is not specified elsewhere. Расслышать is perfective; расслышивать is an imperfective partner. Finite forms include расслышу and расслышишь. It takes an accusative object and here reports failure to hear it clearly. A polite explanation for asking someone to repeat a name. |
| ru-03-1310 | B1n | thank; First person singular present. Благодарить is imperfective; поблагодарить is its perfective partner for a completed act of thanks. Finite forms include благодарю and благодаришь. It takes an accusative person and can stand as a present-time expression of thanks. A relatively formal response suitable in a service encounter. |
| ru-03-1311 | B1n | great; An informal approving response to a friend's offer. |
| ru-03-1312 | B1n | no problem; Без governs the genitive plural проблем. An informal reassurance to a friend that the requested change is acceptable. |
| ru-03-1313 | B1n | no harm done; Страшного is genitive singular neuter after ничего. Reassures someone who has apologized that the problem is not serious. |
| ru-03-1314 | B1n | point of view; Точку is accusative singular feminine; зрения is genitive singular. A neutral expression for someone's position in a discussion. In the reply, understanding a point of view acknowledges it without stating agreement. |
| ru-03-1315 | B1n | so; Introduces the listener's inference from the stated closure as a request for confirmation. |
| ru-03-1316 | B1n | reachable; На governs prepositional singular связи. Tells the friend that the writer is available to be contacted. |
| ru-03-1317 | B1n | in writing; Specifies the form in which the speaker wants to receive the address. |
| ru-03-1318 | B1n | chat for a while; First person plural future, used with давай to make a suggestion. Поболтать is perfective, a bounded use related to imperfective болтать. Finite forms include поболтаю and поболтаешь. It takes no direct object here; the future form proposes a period of conversation. A casual choice for a friend's message. |
| ru-03-1319 | B1n | kind regards; С governs the instrumental singular уважением. A conventional formal letter closing placed before the sender's name. |
| ru-03-1320 | B1n | talk to you later; До governs the genitive singular связи. A friendly closing that suggests future contact without fixing its time. |
| ru-03-1321 | B1n | in other words; Говоря is an imperfective adverbial participle in a fixed parenthetical expression. Говорить is imperfective; сказать is a contextual perfective partner. Present forms include говорю and говорит. Говорить can take an accusative object or a content clause; here говоря forms a fixed comment on the restatement. Introduces an equivalent explanation of free admission. |
| ru-03-1322 | B1n | clarify; Perfective infinitive following можно. Perfective уточнить; imperfective уточнять. Conjugation includes уточню, уточните. It can take a direct object or, as here, an indirect question; the infinitive asks permission to obtain a specific detail. The speaker politely requests a precise detail from a service provider. |
| ru-03-1323 | B1n | thanks in advance; Thanks Masha in advance for the requested schedule, which she has yet to send. |
| ru-03-1324 | B1n | a moment; Accusative singular feminine, expressing the requested duration of the wait. With подожди, минутку asks for a short wait without specifying an exact minute. |
| ru-03-1325 | B1n | in a little more detail; Comparative adverb with the prefix по-, modifying расскажите. The prefix по- softens the request for a more detailed explanation. |
| ru-03-1326 | B1n | what did you say; Сказали is a plural past form used with polite вы. Perfective сказать; imperfective говорить. Conjugation includes скажу, скажете. Here как asks for repetition of the wording, and the past form refers to the utterance just heard. A polite request to repeat wording that was not understood. |
| ru-03-1327 | B1n | whether correctly; The interrogative particle ли follows the focused adverb правильно. Правильно ли я понимаю asks the listener to confirm the speaker's understanding. |
| ru-03-1328 | B1n | meant; Имела is feminine singular past; masculine имел is also possible for a male speaker. Imperfective иметь в виду has no simple aspectual partner in this sense. Conjugation includes имею в виду, имеешь в виду. It takes an accusative object and here clarifies the intended day. The construction corrects what the speaker intended to say. |
| ru-03-1329 | B1n | meant to say; Хотела is feminine singular past, matching the speaker's сказала. Imperfective хотеть has no simple aspectual partner; its forms include хочу and хочешь. It governs the perfective infinitive сказать, whose contextual imperfective partner is говорить; the phrase corrects an intended utterance. Finite forms of сказать include скажу and скажешь. The speaker repairs her own mistaken word. |
| ru-03-1330 | B1n | understood correctly; Понял is masculine singular past; поняла is accepted for a female speaker. Perfective понять; imperfective понимать. Conjugation includes пойму, поймёшь. It takes an accusative object or a content clause; here the following clause states the speaker's understanding. The conditional opening checks an interpretation without presenting it as certain. |
| ru-03-1331 | B1n | unfortunately; К governs the dative singular noun сожалению. The speaker uses this phrase to introduce unwelcome information politely. |
| ru-03-1332 | B1n | I'm afraid; First person singular present, reflexive. Бояться is imperfective; this speech formula has no simple perfective partner. Finite forms include боюсь and боится. Here the first-person present introduces a regrettable situation in a following clause, rather than literal fright. The opening softens an unwelcome answer to a customer. |
| ru-03-1333 | B1n | of course; In this formal letter to a client, Разумеется reinforces the assurance that the contract will be sent. |
| ru-03-1334 | B1n | of course; A neutral, willing assurance to someone addressed familiarly. |
| ru-03-1335 | B1n | it's agreed; Perfective past plural reflexive form used as a response. Perfective договориться; imperfective договариваться. Conjugation includes договорюсь, договоримся. Договориться о чём takes о with the prepositional; here the agreed arrangement is stated in the preceding question. A familiar response confirming an arrangement. |
| ru-03-1336 | B1n | agree; Short feminine singular adjective; masculine согласен is accepted for a male speaker. Согласна with an infinitive expresses willingness to do the proposed action. |
| ru-03-1337 | B1n | don't mind; Не против followed by an если-clause expresses lack of objection to the proposed action. |
| ru-03-1338 | B1n | will be possible; Third person singular future, reflexive, used impersonally. Получиться is perfective; получаться is its contextual imperfective partner. It belongs to the second conjugation. This impersonal use has third-person singular получится and past neuter получилось, with an infinitive naming the possible action; plural forms from other uses are not examples of this construction. The impersonal construction offers a gentle refusal of a proposed meeting. |
| ru-03-1339 | B1n | invitation; Accusative singular neuter inanimate after за. Спасибо за приглашение acknowledges an invitation before declining it. |
| ru-03-1340 | B1n | hurtful; Impersonal predicative; the dative мне identifies the person who feels hurt. The что-clause names the situation that hurt the speaker's feelings. |
| ru-03-1341 | B1n | in my opinion; The speaker presents the suggestion as a personal opinion. |
| ru-03-1342 | B1n | in my view; На governs the accusative singular masculine noun взгляд; мой agrees with it. The phrase frames the criticism as the speaker's assessment. |
| ru-03-1343 | B1n | a compromise; Accusative singular inanimate masculine; direct object of нашли. Найти компромисс means to reach a solution acceptable to people with different preferences. |
| ru-03-1344 | B1n | not entirely; Не совсем qualifies согласна and softens disagreement with the other person's idea. |
| ru-03-1345 | B1n | on the one hand; С governs genitive singular стороны; одной agrees with it. Introduces one aspect of the situation before a contrasting consideration. |
| ru-03-1346 | B1n | I sympathize; First-person singular present. Imperfective сочувствовать has no simple perfective partner in this sense. Present forms include сочувствую and сочувствует; сочувствовать кому takes the dative. The present form offers sympathy now. A sympathetic response to the other speaker's setback. |
| ru-03-1347 | B1n | thank you for your understanding; Благодарю is first-person singular present; за governs accusative singular понимание. Imperfective благодарить; perfective поблагодарить. Present forms include благодарю and благодарит. Благодарить кого за что takes an accusative person and за with the accusative; the present form performs the thanks. A formal closing acknowledging the visitors' understanding of the necessary closure. |
| ru-03-1348 | B1n | it is said; Third person singular present reflexive form used impersonally. Говориться is imperfective, second conjugation, with no simple perfective partner in this text-topic use; сказаться belongs to other senses. The impersonal reporting form is third-person singular говорится, past neuter говорилось. О with prepositional names the topic discussed by the text. Говориться о plus a topic presents what a text discusses, rather than quoting an assertion in a что-clause. |
| ru-03-1349 | B1n | likewise; A response indicating that the speaker shares the other person's pleasant feeling about meeting. |
| ru-03-1350 | B1n | as far as I know; Знаю is first person singular present. Imperfective знать has no simple aspectual partner in the sense of possessing knowledge. Conjugation includes знаю, знаешь. It can take an accusative object or a content clause; this fixed introduction limits the claim to the speaker's knowledge. The phrase signals that the following information may not be complete. |
| ru-03-1351 | B1n | send; Perfective infinitive following можешь. Perfective скинуть; contextual imperfective partner скидывать. Conjugation includes скину, скинешь. Скинуть что кому takes an accusative item and a dative recipient; here it requests one message. A colloquial request to a familiar person to send information, often electronically. |
| ru-03-1352 | B1n | we hereby notify; First-person plural present, representing the library as institutional speaker. Imperfective уведомлять; perfective уведомить. Present forms include уведомляю and уведомляет. Уведомлять кого о чём takes an accusative recipient and о with the prepositional; a что-clause can state the content. Here the present form gives formal notice. A formal institutional notification addressed to library readers. |
| ru-03-1353 | B1n | send a quick message; Second-person singular reflexive imperative. Perfective отписаться; imperfective отписываться in this messaging sense. Future forms include отпишусь and отпишешься. Отписаться кому takes a dative recipient; the imperative requests a completed written follow-up. A familiar request for a brief chat message after arrival, not a request to unsubscribe. |
| ru-03-1354 | B1n | I request; First person singular present. Imperfective просить; perfective попросить. Conjugation includes прошу, просите. Просить кого with an infinitive can identify the person asked in the accusative; here the addressee is named separately and the infinitive states the request. Прошу with an infinitive is a formal written request. |
| ru-03-1355 | B1n | grateful; Short feminine singular adjective; masculine признателен is accepted for a male writer. Буду признательна за with an accusative noun is a formal written request for the stated action. |
| ru-03-1356 | B1n | I remind you; First person singular present. Imperfective напоминать; perfective напомнить. Conjugation includes напоминаю, напоминаете. Напоминать кому о чём takes a dative recipient and о with the prepositional; here the following что-clause states the reminder. The speaker reminds colleagues of an existing deadline. |
| ru-03-1357 | B1n | in response to; The expression governs the accusative: ваше письмо. A formal written opening that identifies the message being answered. |
| ru-03-1358 | B1n | by the way; Introduces a question in an informal conversation. |
| ru-03-1359 | B1n | all right; The speaker informally settles on discussing the price later; давай addresses the listener familiarly. |
| ru-03-1360 | B1n | contact; Second person plural imperative, reflexive, suitable for polite singular or plural address. Imperfective обращаться; perfective обратиться. Conjugation includes обращаюсь, обращаетесь. Обращаться к кому takes к with the dative; the imperfective imperative gives a general invitation to seek help if needed. A polite service formula directing customers to a person for help. |
| ru-03-1361 | B1n | specify; Second-person plural imperative, used for polite address. Perfective; imperfective указывать. Future укажу, укажете; takes an accusative object. The imperative requests a completed specification. A neutral instruction to specify the cancellation reason in a letter to a client. |
| ru-03-1362 | B1n | excuse me; Прошу is first-person singular present; прощения is genitive singular. Imperfective просить; perfective попросить. Present forms include прошу and просит. Просить прощения uses the genitive; here the present form introduces a polite interruption. Gets the addressee's attention before asking for directions to the ticket office. |
| ru-03-1363 | B1n | allow; Second-person plural imperative, used for polite singular address. Perfective; imperfective позволять. Future позволю, позволит; takes a dative person and an infinitive. The imperative requests permission for the speaker to explain. A polite request to take a turn and explain. |
| ru-03-1364 | B1n | don't object; Negated second-person plural present, used with polite singular вы. Возражать is imperfective; perfective возразить is used for voicing an objection. Present возражаю, возражает; here an если-clause names the proposed action. The negative question politely asks permission by checking for an objection. |
| ru-03-1365 | B1n | due to; The compound preposition governs the instrumental: ремонтом. Introduces the reason for the closure in a formal notice. |
| ru-03-1366 | B1n | further to; The compound preposition governs the genitive: нашего телефонного разговора. A professional written opening referring back to an earlier conversation. |
| ru-03-1367 | B1n | formal inquiry; Genitive singular neuter after тему. Обращение here is a communication addressed to an organization. |
| ru-03-1368 | B1n | get in touch; Second-person plural reflexive imperative, suitable for polite singular address. Perfective; imperfective связываться. Future свяжусь, свяжется; с governs the instrumental person contacted. The imperative requests a completed contact for the stated purpose. A polite service instruction to contact the administrator about changing an appointment date. |
| ru-03-1369 | B1n | bear in mind; Имейте is a second-person plural imperative, suitable for polite singular or plural address; в виду is fixed. Imperfective иметь has no simple perfective partner in this expression. Present forms include имею and имеет. Имейте в виду can introduce a что-clause naming the fact to remember; the imperative gives notice. Asks the addressee to remember the library's Sunday closure, rather than expressing what someone means. |
| ru-03-1370 | B1n | I apologize; Приношу is first-person singular present; извинения is accusative plural. Приносить is imperfective; the formal perfective expression is принести извинения. Present приношу, приносит; извинения is the accusative object, and за with the accusative names the error. The present performs the apology. A conventional formal apology; the expression is not literal carrying. |
| ru-03-1371 | B1n | great idea; Nominative singular feminine; отличная agrees with идея. An enthusiastic response approving the friend's suggestion. |
| ru-03-1372 | B1n | excellent; Approves the offer before the speaker adds a complementary plan. |
| ru-03-1373 | B1n | quite; A compact affirmative reply: Wednesday is quite convenient. |
| ru-03-1374 | B1n | go ahead and take it; Second-person singular imperfective imperative. Imperfective; perfective взять. Present беру, берёшь; takes an accusative object, understood here. The imperative grants permission in response to the request. A familiar response to someone using твою. |
| ru-03-1375 | B1n | please do; Будьте is a plural imperative; добры is a short plural adjective. Будьте is the imperative of imperfective быть, which has no simple aspectual partner here; future буду, будете. The phrase accepts the offered action. A polite response to the offer to pass the menu. |
| ru-03-1376 | B1n | with pleasure; С governs instrumental singular удовольствием. A willing acceptance of the invitation. |
| ru-03-1377 | B1n | I'll pass; An informal formula for opting out of the friend's invitation. |
| ru-03-1378 | B1n | I'll try; First-person singular perfective future, reflexive. Perfective; imperfective стараться. Future постараюсь, постарается; an infinitive may name the action, understood here from the question. It expresses an intention to make an effort. The qualification makes clear that attendance is not promised. |
| ru-03-1379 | B1n | there's no need; Negated third-person singular present used impersonally. Стоить is imperfective, second conjugation. In this impersonal use it has no simple perfective partner; selected forms are third-person singular стоит and past neuter стоило. The infinitive naming the declined action is understood from the preceding offer. Politely declines the proposed change because the speaker will arrive in time. |
| ru-03-1380 | B1n | apparently; Grammaticalized parenthetical from a third-person singular present reflexive form; it qualifies the following affirmative reply. Derived from imperfective казаться, first conjugation. In this fixed parenthetical use кажется has no simple perfective partner or object; it is not an ordinary predicate of appearance. Marks the affirmative answer as tentative. |
| ru-03-1381 | B1n | correct; Second-person plural imperative, used for polite singular address; меня is the accusative person corrected. Perfective; imperfective поправлять. Future поправлю, поправит; takes an accusative person. The imperative invites a correction if the stated understanding is wrong. |
| ru-03-1382 | B1n | explain; Second-person plural imperative, suitable for polite singular address. Perfective; imperfective пояснять. Future поясню, пояснит; takes an accusative object or a clause stating what needs explanation. The imperative requests an explanation of the phrase. |
| ru-03-1383 | B1n | mean; Second-person plural present, used with polite вы. Imperfective иметь в виду has no simple aspectual partner in this sense; present имею в виду, имеете в виду. It takes an accusative object. The question checks the intended referent rather than asserting it. |
| ru-03-1384 | B1n | is that all correct; Всё is a neuter singular pronoun; верно is a short neuter predicate. A check of the number just read back to the other person. |
| ru-03-1385 | B1n | made a slip of the tongue; Masculine singular reflexive past; сказал identifies the same speaker as male. Perfective оговориться; imperfective оговариваться in this sense. Future forms include оговорюсь and оговорится. The verb can stand without an object; the past form acknowledges a completed slip of speech. The speaker corrects the spoken day Tuesday to the intended day Wednesday. |
| ru-03-1386 | B1n | incorrectly; Modifies указал and acknowledges an error in an earlier message. |
| ru-03-1387 | B1n | that is; Introduces a colloquial restatement of the same approximate time in a chat between friends. |
| ru-03-1388 | B1n | while you're at it; Adds the incidental message about the meeting to the main task of sending the contract. |
| ru-03-1389 | B1n | misunderstood; Negated masculine singular past; feminine не так поняла is also accepted because the speaker's gender is not otherwise specified. Понять is perfective; imperfective понимать. Future пойму, поймёт; it can take an accusative object or a clause. The past acknowledges a completed misunderstanding. The speaker acknowledges a misunderstanding and supplies the correct interpretation. |
| ru-03-1390 | B1n | correction; Nominative singular feminine, used as a standalone notice. Signals that the speaker is correcting the day just stated. |
| ru-03-1391 | B1n | have a phone call; Perfective reflexive infinitive following предложила. Perfective; imperfective созваниваться. Future созвонюсь, созвонимся; с with the instrumental can name the other participant. The infinitive names a proposed completed contact. A common way to suggest a mutual phone call. |
| ru-03-1392 | B1n | let me know; Дай is a second-person singular imperative; знать is an infinitive. Дать is perfective; the corresponding ongoing expression is давать знать. Future дам, даст; the expression can take a dative recipient and a clause stating what to report. The imperative requests a notification. Знать is imperfective, first conjugation, with present forms знаю and знает; it has no simple aspectual partner in this fixed idiom. A familiar request to notify the speaker on reaching home. |
| ru-03-1393 | B1n | no offense; Без governs the genitive plural обид. A familiar softener before a mild criticism; it does not guarantee that the listener will not be offended. |
| ru-03-1394 | B1n | are accompanying; Third-person singular present, used with the quantity phrase сколько гостей. Imperfective; perfective сопроводить. Present сопровождаю, сопровождает; takes the accusative вас. The present identifies those accompanying the addressee. A neutral, fairly formal choice in a message to organizers. |
| ru-03-1395 | B1n | take along; Second-person singular perfective imperative. Perfective; imperfective захватывать in this sense. Future захвачу, захватишь; takes an accusative object. The imperative asks the friend to take the umbrella along. Захватить is a familiar, informal alternative to взять с собой in this context. |
| ru-03-1396 | B1n | I endorse; First-person singular present reflexive form. Imperfective; perfective присоединиться. Present присоединяюсь, присоединяется; к governs the dative of the proposal endorsed. The present performs the endorsement. In a discussion, присоединяться к предложению expresses support for a proposal. |
| ru-03-1397 | B1n | in person; Modifies обсудить: the friends plan to discuss the topic in person. |
| ru-03-1398 | B1n | all the best; Всего and доброго are genitive singular neuter forms in this fixed wish. A polite closing wish at the end of the service exchange. |
| ru-03-1399 | B1n | sounds sensible; Third-person singular present verb with an adverb; the understood subject is план. Звучать is imperfective; perfective прозвучать can describe a single instance. Present звучу, звучит; разумно characterizes the impression made by the plan. A measured positive response to the proposed plan. |
| ru-03-1400 | B1n | my point is that; К governs the dative тому; что introduces the substantive point. Clarifies why the preceding statement about buses matters: the speakers will have to walk. |
